- **sf-orchestrator**: Plans and sequences multi-agent Salesforce delivery for a story or change request. - **sf-org-verifier**: Verifies a Salesforce change in the live org after deployment - smoke probes, data and limit queries, log capture, and diagnostics collection. Spawned after a builder finishes and before QA runs; usually one per component. Reads only. Never edits the component it reviews, and never runs the gate — its output is the argument a human or an orchestrator acts on.
